## request_user_input availability

Use the `request_user_input` tool only when it is listed in the available tools for this turn.

Use the `request_user_input` tool only for optional questions where the answer would materially improve the quality of the work.

If `request_user_input` returns no answers, continue with best judgment instead of asking again or treating the turn as blocked.

## developer

<multi_agent_role>You are `/root`, the primary agent in a team of agents collaborating to fulfill the user's goals.

At the start of your turn, you are the active agent.
You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents.
All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent without triggering a turn.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.
You can decide how much context you want to propagate to your sub-agents with the `fork_turns` parameter.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
They may be addressed as to=/root

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\projects\lsatu-home-v2

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

point of the file.*

# AGENTS.md

This file is the Codex-facing adapter for this repository. It summarizes the Claude-oriented project guidance from `CLAUDE.md` in a smaller format that is fast for Codex to scan. `CLAUDE.md` remains the fuller source of project knowledge.

## Fast Locator

- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`
- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`
- Live vault knowledge: `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/wiki/` (hubs, voice, business), `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/students/`, `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/corpus/`
- Pre-2026-07-16 LSATU product knowledge (archived, read-only): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`

If an older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat it as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.

## Project Map

- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.
- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.
- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.
- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.
- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.
- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.
- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.
- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.

## Stack And Commands

- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.
- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.
- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.
- Build: `npm run build`
- Lint: `npm run lint`
- Test: `npm run test`
- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`
- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.
- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).

## Working Rules

- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.
- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.
- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.
- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.
- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.
- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.
- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.
- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.

## Product Constraints

- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.
- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.
- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.
- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.
- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.

## Smart Drill Note

On `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.

## Verification (must not skip)

- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.
- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.

## Workflow Expectations

- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.
- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.
- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.
- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.
- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.

## Session Protocol (second brain)

Product context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.

The vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student's record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.

- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.
- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong "Done" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.

`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.

## Codex Adapter Layout

- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.
- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.
- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.

This checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.

## Known Gaps

- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.
- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.
- `README.md` is still minimal.

## Agent skills

### Issue tracker

GitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.

### Triage labels

The five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.

### Domain docs

Multi-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.

CONSTRAINTS:

- Preserve: assigned work rows and actions, timed-section briefing interception, report-card seen tracking, message preview dismissal watermark, HomeAccess gates, Next seven days strip byte-for-byte.

The two round-3 fixes were applied after the final round and were not re-reviewed by Codex._\\r\\n\\r\\nDecisions come from Joshua\'s grill-me interview: `brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-visual-upgrade.md`. Visual reference: https://claude.ai/artifact/6zF3FgewiMA7pCZSk766Xt (v1; its PT trend, goal dial, streak, month calendar and week curve are REJECTED).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Situation\\r\\n- App: `projects/lsatu` (React + Vite + TS + Tailwind, TanStack Query present, Supabase via Lovable Cloud). Home = `/classroom` in the Classroom frame (`src/components/classroom/CONTEXT.md`).\\r\\n- Home code: `src/components/classroom/home/` — `useClassroomHome.ts`, `homeModel.ts` (pure, tested), `ClassroomHomeView.tsx`, `home.css`.\\r\\n- ~25 one-to-one students plus Joshua. Student copy follows `wiki/teaching-voice.md`; no em dashes.\\r\\n- Hard rules: completed student work never lost or modified (additive migrations only). Branch per session, PR to main. Lovable Cloud: Edge Functions deploy through Lovable, confirm with Joshua first. Frontend reaches lsatprep.study only via Lovable deploy. A teacher is never the student.\\r\\n- Done separately: PR #132 (sideways scrollbar).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Goal\\r\\nA sleek, high-end Home that (1) gets a student into practice in one click, (2) shows the performance insight that matters most for THIS student\'s recent work, and (3) puts every message and notification in one place.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Approach\\r\\n\\r\\n### 0. Feature-preservation checklist (applies to every phase)\\r\\nThe redesign must keep, until a named replacement ships:\\r\\n- Assigned work rows and their actions (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:168`).\\r\\n- Timed-section link interception that shows the Section briefing (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:197`, `SectionBriefing.tsx`).\\r\\n- Report-card seen tracking (`ClassroomHomeView.tsx:362`, `reportCardSeen.ts`).\\r\\n- Home message preview dismissal watermark.\\r\\n- Access gates in `HomeAccess` (messages, schedule, activities, drill): a hidden feature stays hidden.\\r\\n- Next seven days strip: markup, classes and behavior unchanged.\\r\\nExisting `ClassroomHomeView.test.tsx` cases stay green; new cases added per phase.\\r\\n\\r\\n### 1. Layout\\r\\nTop to bottom; two columns decided by the Home container\'s available width (CSS container query), not the viewport, so a docked side panel collapses it to one column.\\r\\n1. **Practice launcher** (the existing `QuestionFinder`, extended; one instance, one Ctrl/Cmd+K owner, lazy bank loading and focus trap kept) + inbox bell + greeting.\\r\\n2. **Three one-click actions**:\\r\\n   - Continue assigned work: the earliest-due eligible assigned row (not overdue-dismissed, not teacher-addressed); hidden when none. Timed sections still route through the briefing.\\r\\n   - Smart Drill: `kind: \'smart-drill\'` (adaptive, unchanged).\\r\\n   - Start a timed section: opens the launcher on its Section tab.\\r\\n3. **Performance area**: one main insight + up to three minor ones (fewer when evidence is thin).\\r\\n4. **Inbox panel** (right column): unified feed, Joshua\'s human-written messages pinned.\\r\\n5. **Next session** card (dark).\\r\\n6. **Next seven days**: unchanged.\\r\\n- Look: light and warm, dark cards for emphasis. Motion: count-up, settle-in, gentle pulse; off under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\\r\\n- Empty states: one line + one action.\\r\\n- Verification matrix: student account at narrow (390), mid (900), wide (1440), and side-panel-docked widths; keyboard-only pass; reduced motion; dark class (`html.dark`).\\r\\n\\r\\n### 2. Practice launcher (Joshua\'s \\"specific question / dropdown\\" idea)\\r\\nOne panel, three tabs, all through the existing launch codec (`src/lib/drillLaunch.ts`):\\r\\n- **Question**: PrepTest → Section → Question dropdowns plus free text (exists). Contextual action changes with depth: PT+Section selected → \\"Start section\\"; +Question → \\"Open question\\". LR/RC routing stays the finder\'s.\\r\\n- **Section**: bank-aware dispatch: an LR section launches `kind: \'section\'` (LR bank, `Drill.tsx:540`); an RC section does NOT reuse the finder\'s single-passage link (`passage=<id>` opens one passage with `DEFAULT_CONFIG`, `RcPlayer.tsx:130`). New RC section launch: resolve every passage of that PT section in order, create an RC session with those passage ids and the chosen timer config (the player already accepts multiple ids and explicit config, `RcPlayer.tsx:141`), then navigate by session id. PrepTest + Section, annotated with freshness (\\"24 of 25 unanswered\\", `sectionFreshness.ts:46`), fresh-only toggle, and the student\'s timer setting (standard / 52.5 / 70 / custom / untimed) carried into `kind: \'section\'` `timerMode`. RC freshness shown as unknown until RC history is connected.\\r\\n- **By type**: question-type multi-select + count + optional difficulty → `kind: \'filter\'` (`drillLaunch.ts:163`). Adaptive Smart Drill is never repurposed for this. \\"Fresh only\\" needs an exposure-aware path: add an optional `excludeAnswered?: boolean` to the filter body (additive, old links unaffected), resolved when the queue is built (`drillRunSpec.ts:146`) against the student\'s answered qids; the built queue is stored in the Run Record so resume replays the same questions. If fewer fresh questions exist than `count`, say \\"Only N new Flaw questions left\\" before start, with an option to include answered ones.\\r\\n- Shelves (search-matched in the same panel): Flagged (`flaggedService.ts`) and Needs review (journal review status). Decided: yes (Joshua, 2026-09-23).\\r\\n\\r\\n### 3. Insight engine (fixed rules, no AI text)\\r\\nPure `pickInsights(signals, pin?)` in `src/components/classroom/home/insights.ts`, fixture-tested. Adapters feed it, one per source; the engine never reads Supabase.\\r\\n\\r\\nSource-to-lens matrix:\\r\\n| Lens | Source / reader | Coverage label |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| LR question types | NEW additive RPC `home_lr_type_accuracy(p_class_id, p_days int default 30)`: latest attempt per distinct question in window, excluding review/blind-review replays; returns qtype, questions, correct, last_attempt_at. Companion RPC `home_lr_type_evidence(p_class_id, p_qtype, p_days)` shares the SAME selection function, returning the qids behind the number | \\"LR practice, last 30 days\\" |\\r\\n| Sections | Home adapter over `section_history`, NOT the unchanged `sectionHistory.ts` reader (it zero-fills missing `initial_total`/`initial_percent`/`total_time_ms` and stamps missing dates as now, `sectionHistory.ts:113`). Adapter derives totals from `total_questions`/`initial_score`, leaves unknown dates unknown (excluded from recency), never infers timing | \\"Sections\\" (\\"Timed\\" only when `mode` proves it) |\\r\\n| Section diagnosis | saved `analysis_json` via `sectionAnalysis.ts` categories (reading / fundamentals / process / test-taking); Home summarizes the top existing finding and links to its report | \\"From your last section\\" |\\r\\n| RC | `src/features/rc/api/rcProgress.ts` | \\"Reading Comprehension\\" |\\r\\n| Bootcamp | per-topic progress stores (e.g. `topics/*/lib/supabase-progress.ts`) | \\"Bootcamp: <name>\\" |\\r\\n| Assigned work | `useAssignedTodoFeed` status only: done / due / overdue counts. No scores inferred from copy | \\"Work Joshua assigned\\" |\\r\\n| Repeat mistakes | `wrong_answer_journal` by qtype; trap wording from `wajNotesService` notes where present | \\"Wrong Answer Journal\\" |\\r\\n| Pace / confidence | `attempts.time_ms`, `attempts.confidence` (LR only) | \\"LR practice\\" |\\r\\n\\r\\nSelection rules:\\r\\n- Each lens returns `null` (ineligible) or `{ score 0..1, evidence }`. Eligibility floors per lens (e.g. qtype: ≥8 distinct questions; sections: ≥1 completed in 21 days; RC: ≥2 passages in 21 days).\\r\\n- Score = 0.5 × recency (days since last activity in that source, decays over 14 days) + 0.5 × actionability (size of the gap the lens found, normalized per lens). Volume never compared across units.\\r\\n- Ties broken by a fixed lens order. Redundancy: at most one card per source; a section cannot feed both Sections and Section diagnosis unless the diagnosis names something the score card does not.\\r\\n- 0 eligible → a single \\"Start here\\" card (Smart Drill). 1–3 eligible → show only those.\\r\\n- Pinned lens becomes main if eligible; if ineligible, show it as main with its empty state + the engine\'s picks as minors, and flag it in Joshua\'s Hub as \\"pinned view has no data\\".\\r\\n- Every insight has: coverage label, sample size, date range, an **evidence drawer**, and actions. Evidence is source-specific: question addresses for LR types (evidence RPC) and sections (`questions_json`); module list for bootcamp; the assignment rows for assigned work; passage list for RC.\\r\\n- From a section diagnosis, \\"Practice this exact skill\\": when the finding\'s type/feature ids match launch filters (`sectionAnalysis.ts:160` to `kind: \'filter\'` `qtypes`/`qfeatures`), prefill and show the filters before launch. For a weak LR type: \\"Try new ones\\" (`kind: \'filter\'`, fresh only) and, when journal entries exist, \\"Review these\\" (journal). Pairing decided: yes (Joshua, 2026-09-23).\\r\\n\\r\\n### 4. Pinning\\r\\nAdditive table `home_insight_pins`: `class_id text primary key references profiles(class_id)`, `lens text not null check (lens in (<enum>))`, `params jsonb not null default \'{}\'`, `pinned_by uuid not null default auth.uid()`, `updated_at timestamptz default now()`.\\r\\nRLS: admin SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE (via existing admin role check); student SELECT where `class_id` = their own profile\'s class_id. Params validated per lens in the client and by a CHECK on known keys. Unpin = delete row. Hub control on the student\'s page. RLS tests: student cannot read another student\'s pin or write their own.\\r\\n\\r\\n### 5. Unified inbox\\r\\n- New, separately named hook `useHomeFeed()` (existing `useInbox` untouched), on TanStack Query keys shared by Home panel and bell.\\r\\n- **Event identity**: additive nullable `event_key text` on BOTH `notifications` and individual `messages`, plus `origin text check in (\'human\',\'system\')` on individual messages (not threads). Generated writers (`classroomNotify.ts:87`, `sessionBundleNotify.ts:48`, new writers) compute one deterministic key per event (e.g. `assignment:<id>:assigned`) and insert both rows with it; partial unique indexes `(recipient, event_key) where event_key is not null` on each table make retries no-ops. Human-typed messages get `origin=\'human\'`, no key.\\r\\n- **Protection**: extend the notifications immutability trigger (`20260506124235_...sql:120`) to cover `event_key`/`origin`, and the same on messages; tests prove a student cannot change either.\\r\\n- **One canonical visible-event model**, computed server-side in one RPC `home_feed(p_limit, p_before)`: an event with a key appears once (message wins, notification suppressed); legacy rows without keys use the 5-minute same-link heuristic inside the same RPC. The badge count comes from the same RPC, so counts never include hidden rows.\\r\\n- **Read**: two separate read states, never mixed. (a) Conversation read = the existing participant `last_read_at` watermark (`useInbox.ts:247`), advanced ONLY when the student opens the conversation itself. (b) Generated events = a new additive `event_acks (user_id, event_key, acked_at)` table; opening an assignment/recap/grade/reminder event acks its key, which hides its notification duplicate and marks the generated message as read for the feed, without moving the conversation watermark. A generated message with no notification twin is acked the same way. Unread for a thread row = unread human messages after the watermark; generated messages count as unread until acked. Mark-all-read acks visible events and advances watermarks only for threads shown. RLS: student reads/writes own acks only.\\r\\n- **Thread rows**: preview is Joshua\'s latest unread human message when one exists, else the latest message; unread = any unread human message from Joshua in the thread, not only the latest (the latest-only check in `useInbox.ts:189` is not reused).\\r\\n- Hidden types (`streak`, `signup`) filtered in SQL before paging; page size 30 with load more.\\r\\n- New notification types need the DB CHECK constraint extended (additive). Writers:\\r\\n  | Event | Writer | Status |\\r\\n  |---|---|---|\\r\\n  | New assignment | `classroomNotify.ts` | exists, add key/origin |\\r\\n  | Notes / recaps | `sessionBundleNotify.ts` | exists, add key/origin |\\r\\n  | Graded work | grading completion (`activity_section_grades` flow) | new |\\r\\n  | Session reminder | none today (type defined, no producer, verified). New scheduled job: 24h and 1h before `start_at`; key `session:<booking_id>:<offset>`; skipped when cancelled or moved, re-keyed on reschedule | new; needs a scheduler (Edge Function cron via Lovable, confirm with Joshua) |\\r\\n  | Booking change | booking Edge Function | new, Lovable deploy |\\r\\n\\r\\n### 6. Build order (each its own PR, each passes the preservation checklist)\\r\\n1. Layout + restyle, existing data only; launcher tabs Question + Section; actions; inbox panel showing today\'s message preview; Next seven days untouched.\\r\\n2. Insight engine: LR types (new RPC), Sections, Section diagnosis; evidence drawer.\\r\\n3. Unified feed + bell + dedup columns + notification query fixes.\\r\\n4. RC, bootcamp, assigned, mistakes, pace/confidence lenses; By-type launcher tab; pinning.\\r\\n5. New notification writers: graded work; session reminders (scheduled job); booking changes. Reminders and booking need Lovable Edge Function deploys, confirmed with Joshua first.\\r\\n6. Teacher view adopts the design.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Key decisions & tradeoffs\\r\\n- Rules, not AI text: predictable, never states a false thing.\\r\\n- Source adapters + coverage labels instead of one \\"all practice\\" number: honest, more code.\\r\\n- Separate `useHomeFeed` instead of rewriting `useInbox`: zero risk to current consumers, some duplication.\\r\\n- Dedup via additive `event_key`/`origin` + a legacy heuristic, instead of back-filling old rows: no data changes.\\r\\n- Container-query layout instead of viewport breakpoints.\\r\\n- Next seven days frozen.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Risks / open questions\\r\\n- Performance: Home adds RPC + section history + feed. Plan: parallel queries, skeletons, no waterfall; measure first contentful paint before/after on a student account.\\r\\n- Legacy dedup heuristic may miss or over-hide; accept for legacy only.\\r\\n- Exact decay and floor values need tuning on real student data (fixtures from 3 anonymized students).\\r\\n- Product decisions (Joshua, 2026-09-23: yes to all three): Flagged and Needs review shelves in the launcher, Needs review ordered by the student\'s reattempt_due_at with next_action shown; Review these next to Try new ones on weak-area insights; session reminders 24h and 1h before, via a scheduled job (Lovable deploy, confirm the route before deploying).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Out of scope\\r\\n- Public landing page. Goal score, streaks, latest PT, recent drills list, month calendar. AI insight text. Changes to how Smart Drill or assignments run.\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Classroom Home, presentational half.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Takes a finished model and renders it. Split from the live hook so the whole\\r\\n * page can be driven with fixtures in a test, and so the only thing that can\\r\\n * put a number on this screen is the model.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Layout follows the design handoff: masthead, hero, a two-column bento of\\r\\n * assigned work against the coach, then the week band. (\\r\\n        <button\\r\\n          type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n          className=\\"classroom-home-done\\"\\r\\n          disabled={completing}\\r\\n          aria-label={`Mark ${row.title} as done`}\\r\\n          onClick={() => onMarkDone(row.key)}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          {completing ? \\"Saving…\\" : \\"Mark done\\"}\\r\\n        </button>\\r\\n      ) : null}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction MessagePreviewBody({ message }: { message: HomeMessage }) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <span className=\\"classroom-home-message-body\\">\\r\\n      <span className=\\"classroom-home-message-head\\">\\r\\n        <b>{message.from}</b>\\r\\n        <time>{message.timeLabel}</time>\\r\\n      </span>\\r\\n      <span className=\\"classroom-home-message-text\\">{`“${message.body}”`}</span>\\r\\n    </span>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction MessagePreviewButton({\\r\\n  message,\\r\\n  onOpen,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  message: HomeMessage;\\r\\n  onOpen: (route: string) => void;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <button\\r\\n      type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n      className=\\"classroom-home-message\\"\\r\\n      onClick={() => onOpen(message.route)}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      <i className=\\"classroom-home-shine\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n      <span className=\\"classroom-home-avatar\\">\\r\\n        {message.initial}\\r\\n        {message.unread ? (\\r\\n          <i className=\\"classroom-home-avatar-dot\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n        ) : null}\\r\\n      </span>\\r\\n      <MessagePreviewBody message={message} />\\r\\n    </button>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function ClassroomHomeView({\\r\\n  model,\\r\\n  loading,\\r\\n  onOpen,\\r\\n  onMarkDone,\\r\\n  onClearMessage,\\r\\n  completingKey,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  model: ClassroomHomeModel;\\r\\n  loading: boolean;\\r\\n  onOpen: (route: string) => void;\\r\\n  onMarkDone?: (key: string) => void;\\r\\n  onClearMessage?: (conversationId: string) => void;\\r\\n  completingKey?: string | null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  /** Kept for callers; the hero no longer duplicates the sidebar\'s Assigned work link. */\\r\\n  onSeeAllWork?: () => void;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const { hero } = model;\\r\\n  const [briefing, setBriefing] = React.useState<SectionBriefingData | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * A timed section is the one kind of assigned work whose page was only ever\\r\\n   * instructions. (\\r\\n        <SectionBriefing\\r\\n          briefing={briefing}\\r\\n          onStart={() => onOpen(briefing.href)}\\r\\n          onDismiss={() => setBriefing(null)}\\r\\n        />\\r\\n      ) : null}\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* ── finder bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n          The first thing above the fold, the way LSAT Lab puts Find A Question\\r\\n          at the top of its home. (\\r\\n            <span className=\\"classroom-home-duepill\\">\\r\\n              <i aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n              {model.dueTodayCount === 1\\r\\n                ? \\"1 DUE TODAY\\"\\r\\n                : `${model.dueTodayCount} DUE TODAY`}\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n          ) : null}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </header>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* ── hero ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\\r\\n      {hero ? (\\r\\n        <section className=\\"classroom-home-hero\\" aria-label=\\"Pick up where you left off\\">\\r\\n          <i className=\\"classroom-home-sheet classroom-home-sheet-back\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n          <i className=\\"classroom-home-sheet classroom-home-sheet-front\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n          <div className=\\"classroom-home-hero-panel\\">\\r\\n            <i className=\\"classroom-home-hero-glow\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n            <div className=\\"classroom-home-hero-body\\">\\r\\n              <div className=\\"classroom-home-hero-main\\">\\r\\n                <span className=\\"classroom-home-hero-eyebrow\\">\\r\\n                  <i aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n                  {hero.eyebrow}\\r\\n                </span>\\r\\n                <div className=\\"classroom-home-hero-heading\\">\\r\\n                  <h2>{hero.row.title}</h2>\\r\\n                  <span className=\\"classroom-home-hero-meta\\">\\r\\n                    <span className=\\"classroom-home-tag\\">{hero.kindLabel}</span>\\r\\n                    {hero.meta}\\r\\n                  </span>\\r\\n                </div>\\r\\n                {hero.prose ? });\\r\\n\\r\\n  // The masthead runs straight into the hero, the way it did before the\\r\\n  // picker was inserted between them.\\r\\n  it(\\"puts the hero directly under the greeting\\", () => expect(dueLabel(\\"2026-08-03\\", now)).toBe(\\"due today\\");\\r\\n    expect(dueLabel(\\"2026-08-04\\", now)).toBe(\\"due tomorrow\\");\\r\\n    expect(dueLabel(\\"2026-08-02\\", now)).toBe(\\"one day overdue\\");\\r\\n    expect(dueLabel(null, now)).toBeNull();\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"drops the kind from the hero meta, and only when it leads\\", () => {\\r\\n    expect(stripKind(\\"Activity · twelve prompts\\", \\"activity\\")).toBe(\\"twelve prompts\\");\\r\\n    expect(stripKind(\\"twelve prompts · due today\\", \\"activity\\")).toBe(\\r\\n      \\"twelve prompts · due today\\",\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"builds a subtitle from counts, and stays honest at zero\\", () => {\\r\\n    expect(subtitleFor(1, 3, 0)).toBe(\\r\\n      \\"One assignment is due today. Two more are waiting.\\",\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    expect(subtitleFor(0, 0, 0)).toBe(\\r\\n      \\"Nothing is due today. Drill whatever you feel like.\\",\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    expect(subtitleFor(0, 0, 2)).toContain(\\"Two unread messages.\\");\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"rolls the strip forward from today rather than from Monday\\", () => {\\r\\n    // 2026-08-06 is a Thursday.\\r\\n    const dates = weekDates(new Date(2026, 7, 6));\\r\\n    expect(dates[0]).toBe(\\"2026-08-06\\");\\r\\n    expect(dates[6]).toBe(\\"2026-08-12\\");\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"keeps tomorrow on the strip when today is Sunday\\", () => {\\r\\n    // The case a Monday-first week hid: Sunday sat in the last cell, so work\\r\\n    // due Monday night was not on the strip at all.\\r\\n    const dates = weekDates(new Date(2026, 7, 30));\\r\\n    expect(dates[0]).toBe(\\"2026-08-30\\");\\r\\n    expect(dates[1]).toBe(\\"2026-08-31\\");\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"reads an evening deadline as its local day, not its UTC day\\", () => {\\r\\n    // 23:59 on the 30th in New York is 03:59 on the 31st in UTC. Slicing the\\r\\n    // stored timestamp called that tomorrow and cost the row its \\"due today\\"\\r\\n    // rank, so the hero opened the wrong section.\\r\\n    const now = new Date(2026, 7, 30, 9, 0, 0);\\r\\n    expect(dueLabel(\\"2026-08-31T03:59:00+00:00\\", now)).toBe(\\"due today\\");\\r\\n    expect(dueLabel(\\"2026-09-01T03:59:00+00:00\\", now)).toBe(\\"due tomorrow\\");\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"still reads a bare calendar date as itself\\", () => {\\r\\n    const now = new Date(2026, 7, 30, 9, 0, 0);\\r\\n    expect(dueLabel(\\"2026-08-30\\", now)).toBe(\\"due today\\");\\r\\n    expect(dueLabel(\\"2026-08-31\\", now)).toBe(\\"due tomorrow\\");\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"folds several items onto one day without stretching the cell\\", () => {\\r\\n    const dates = weekDates(new Date(2026, 7, 3));\\r\\n    const week = buildWeek(\\r\\n      dates,\\r\\n      [\\r\\n        { iso: \\"2026-08-03\\", label: \\"Negation Station\\" },\\r\\n        { iso: \\"2026-08-03\\", label: \\"Session, 4:00 p.m.\\" },\\r\\n      ],\\r\\n      new Date(2026, 7, 3),\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    expect(week[0].label).toBe(\\"Negation Station +1\\");\\r\\n    expect(week[0].isToday).toBe(true);\\r\\n    expect(week[1].label).toBeNull();\\r\\n    expect(week[5].isWeekend).toBe(true);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n});\\r\\n\\r\\ndescribe(\\"unread notes on Home\\", () => {\\r\\n  const withNote = (isNew: boolean): ClassroomHomeModel => ({\\r\\n    ...EMPTY,\\r\\n    rows: [\\r\\n      {\\r\\n        key: \\"item:n1\\",\\r\\n        title: \\"Causation, Part 1: the argument\\",\\r\\n        kind: \\"recap\\",\\r\\n        tone: \\"recap\\",\\r\\n        meta: \\"Note · posted 19 Aug\\",\\r\\n        route: \\"/classroom/item/n1\\",\\r\\n        actionLabel: \\"Read\\",\\r\\n        emphasis: true,\\r\\n        isNew,\\r\\n        dueToday: false,\\r\\n        overdue: false,\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n    newCount: isNew ? 1 : 0,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"shows an unread note on Home with a Read action, and counts it as new\\", () => {\\r\\n    render(\\r\\n      <ClassroomHomeView\\r\\n        model={withNote(true)}\\r\\n        loading={false}\\r\\n        onOpen={() => {}}\\r\\n        onSeeAllWork={() => {}}\\r\\n      />,\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByText(\\"Causation, Part 1: the argument\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByText(\\"1 NEW\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\r\\n    expect(screen.getAllByText(\\"Read\\").length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"drops the new badge once the note has been opened\\", () => {\\r\\n    render(\\r\\n      <ClassroomHomeView\\r\\n        model={withNote(false)}\\r\\n        loading={false}\\r\\n        onOpen={() => {}}\\r\\n        onSeeAllWork={() => {}}\\r\\n      />,\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByText(\\"Causation, Part 1: the argument\\")).toBeInTheDocument();\\r\\n    expect(screen.queryByText(\\"1 NEW\\")).not.toBeInTheDocument();\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"opens the note at its own route\\", () => {\\r\\n    const onOpen = vi.fn();\\r\\n    render(\\r\\n      <ClassroomHomeView\\r\\n        model={withNote(true)}\\r\\n        loading={false}\\r\\n        onOpen={onOpen}\\r\\n        onSeeAllWork={() => {}}\\r\\n      />,\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getAllByText(\\"Read\\")[0]);\\r\\n    expect(onOpen).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\\"/classroom/item/n1\\");\\r\\n  });\\r\\n});\\r\\n# Classroom\\r\\n\\r\\nThe student\'s own room: the first surface a 1:1 student lands on. It is a reading surface, not a workspace. Its language is second person, and the coach is never named in the furniture.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Terms\\r\\n\\r\\n**Classroom frame** — the persistent layout every Classroom route is drawn in: the cream left rail, an optional middle list, and the white reading surface. Implemented by `ClassroomLayout`. Navigating between surfaces it draws swaps the content only and never remounts the rail. A page \\"sits in the Classroom frame\\" regardless of its URL; `/flagged` and `/journal` both do.\\r\\n\\r\\n**StudentShell** — the older, separate frame with its own 240px rail, still drawing `/mainhub`, `/practice` and the rest. A student moving between a Classroom-frame page and a StudentShell page sees the whole page rebuild. Reducing that boundary is ongoing work, one surface at a time.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Reading column** — the white content column. Most routes use one reading column on the right. A Wrong answers detail uses a four-column Classroom frame: cream rail, entries list, vertical question, and review panel. Below its journal-only breakpoints the entries list becomes an overlay and the review panel moves under the question.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Drill landing workspace** — the pre-question surface at `/classroom/drill`, drawn inside the Classroom frame. It includes drill selection, launch controls, practice insights, and today\'s work; it excludes the official question-answering experience.\\r\\n_Avoid_: Drill page (when the question experience could be confused with the landing surface), Mainhub\\r\\n\\r\\n**Question finder** — the one control that turns something a student can say about a question into that question opened. It takes free text, matched against question text, and it takes a question address (PT 90, Section 1, Question 12) typed or picked from PrepTest / Section / Question selectors. It searches both banks and groups its results by bank, Logical Reasoning first, because a hit lands differently in each: an LR hit opens that one question, an RC hit opens its whole passage with every question on it. The finder is a finding aid, not the scope of the work it opens. It appears on Classroom Home and in the Drill landing workspace; it never builds a set, it hands that to the Drill console.\\r\\n_Avoid_: search bar, command palette, spotlight, quick access, find bar\\r\\n\\r\\n**Drill console** — the control surface inside the Drill landing workspace where a student assembles a set: the filters, the preview of the exact set, and the start action. Finding one named question is not its job; that is the Question finder.\\r\\n_Avoid_: Control pane, picker card\\r\\n\\r\\n**Drill canvas** — the route-specific visual composition to the right of the persistent Classroom rail, containing the Drill hero, Drill console, practice/insight workspace, and Today focus. The Classroom rail is not part of the canvas.\\r\\n_Avoid_: Drill side pane, dashboard grid\\r\\n\\r\\n**Section** — a middle-column list paired with the reading column: Notes and recaps, Assigned work, Flagged. A section is picked in the rail, lists its items in the middle column, and shows the chosen item in the reading column.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Flag** — a come-back-to-this marker the student raises *during* a question, from a drill or a blind review. It records intent to return, and says nothing about whether the question was answered correctly. Stored in `flagged_questions`.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Flagged question** — a question carrying a flag. The Flagged section is the student\'s unfinished business: questions they chose to set aside. Clearing a flag is the student\'s own act (Unflag), not something the system does when they get the question right.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Wrong answer** — a question the student actually missed, recorded in `wrong_answer_journal` and worked through in Wrong answers at `/journal`. Distinct from a flag in both meaning and storage: a flag is chosen, a wrong answer is earned. A question can be both, one, or neither. Do not treat the two lists as views of the same thing.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Journal entry** — the review of one wrong answer at `/journal/:qid`. The URL is the selection: it resolves only by `qid`, never by the journal row id, and `/journal` does not silently select the first entry. Its review fields autosave without a visible save state.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Ask** — sending a question to the coach from the surface the student is looking at, with their own comment attached. Always labelled \\"Ask\\", never with the coach\'s name; the name belongs in messaging itself, not in the furniture that leads to it.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Boundaries\\r\\n\\r\\nThe Classroom owns presentation and the student\'s own annotations of their work. It does not own the work: assignments, drills, attempts and the question bank all belong to their own contexts and are read here, not authored here.\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Warning: truncated output (original token count: 16190)\nTotal output lines: 2\n\n{"chunk_id":"7630ef","wall_time_seconds":1.8411357000000002,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":7296,"output":"/**\\r\\n * The Classroom Home model.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The page reads five things and states them once each: what to pick up, what\\r\\n * is assigned, what the coach said, when the next session is, and what the week\\r\\n * holds. Everything here is derived from work that actually exists in the\\r\\n * student\'s own record. Nothing on this page may be invented: a count, a due\\r\\n * date, or a progress bar that cannot be traced to a row is omitted, not\\r\\n * guessed. The view has honest empty variants for every block.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { localDayISO, parseLocalDay } from \\"@/lib/localDate\\";\\r\\nimport type { SectionBriefing } from \\"@/lib/sectionBriefing\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type HomeKind =\\r\\n  | \\"activity\\"\\r\\n  | \\"guided\\"\\r\\n  | \\"homework\\"\\r\\n  | \\"ta\\"\\r\\n  | \\"bootcamp\\"\\r\\n  | \\"plan\\"\\r\\n  | \\"recap\\"\\r\\n  | \\"note\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Which of the three status dots a row carries. */\\r\\nexport type HomeTone = \\"activity\\" | \\"drill\\" | \\"recap\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeRow {\\r\\n  key: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  kind: HomeKind;\\r\\n  tone: HomeTone;\\r\\n  /** \\"Activity · twelve prompts · due today\\" */\\r\\n  meta: string;\\r\\n  route: string;\\r\\n  /** \\"Open\\" for work, \\"Read\\" for a recap or note. */\\r\\n  actionLabel: string;\\r\\n  /** Orange pill (the one thing to do next) versus the muted ring. */\\r\\n  emphasis: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Never opened. Drives the \\"N NEW\\" badge; derived, never stored twice. */\\r\\n  isNew: boolean;\\r\\n  dueToday: boolean;\\r\\n  overdue: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Work the student can close out by hand; reading matter cannot. */\\r\\n  canComplete?: boolean;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * A timed section\'s instructions, carried on the row so Home can raise them\\r\\n   * over the stage rather than sending the student to a page to read them.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  briefing?: SectionBriefing;\\r\\n\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeProgress {\\r\\n  /** Present only when both halves are provable. */\\r\\n  completed: number | null;\\r\\n  total: number;\\r\\n  /** \\"prompts\\", \\"questions\\" — plural noun for the count. */\\r\\n  unit: string;\\r\\n  estimateMinutes: number | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeHero {\\r\\n  row: HomeRow;\\r\\n  eyebrow: string;\\r\\n  /** \\"ACTIVITY\\", \\"GUIDED DRILL\\" — the mono tag beside the meta line. */\\r\\n  kindLabel: string;\\r\\n  /** The row\'s meta with the kind dropped: the tag beside it already says it. */\\r\\n  meta: string;\\r\\n  prose: string | null;\\r\\n  primaryLabel: string;\\r\\n  progress: HomeProgress | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeMessage {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  from: string;\\r\\n  initial: string;\\r\\n  timeLabel: string;\\r\\n  body: string;\\r\\n  unread: boolean;\\r\\n  route: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeSession {\\r\\n  /** ISO start. Null renders the honest \\"Nothing booked\\" variant. */\\r\\n  startsAt: string;\\r\\n  durationMin: number | null;\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  meta: string | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The plan-aware booking link shown next to the session card. */\\r\\nexport interface HomeBookingCta {\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  href: string;\\r\\n  external: boolean;\\r\\n  detail: string | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeWeekDay {\\r\\n  iso: string;\\r\\n  abbr: string;\\r\\n  dayNum: number;\\r\\n  isToday: boolean;\\r\\n  isWeekend: boolean;\\r\\n  /** The single line under the date, or null for an empty day. */\\r\\n  label: string | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * What this particular person can actually reach.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Home is a shortcut into surfaces the rail already gates, so it has to gate\\r\\n * itself the same way: a student without chat access must not be shown a\\r\\n * message card that dead-ends on a locked route, and one without schedule\\r\\n * access gets a week band that reads but does not click. Admins pass every\\r\\n * check, exactly as they do in the rail.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface HomeAccess {\\r\\n  messages: boolean;\\r\\n  schedule: boolean;\\r\\n  activities: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Gates the Question finder. Without it there is nothing to find. */\\r\\n  drill: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface ClassroomHomeModel {\\r\\n  greeting: string;\\r\\n  displayName: string;\\r\\n  /** True when the viewer is staff looking at their own classroom. */\\r\\n  isAdmin: boolean;\\r\\n  access: HomeAccess;\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n  dateline: string;\\r\\n  subtitle: string;\\r\\n  dueTodayCount: number;\\r\\n  hero: HomeHero | null;\\r\\n  rows: HomeRow[];\\r\\n  newCount: number;\\r\\n  message: HomeMessage | null;\\r\\n  /** Every preview-worthy thread, newest first. `message` is its head. */\\r\\n  messages: HomeMessage[];\\r\\n  unreadMessages: number;\\r\\n  session: HomeSession | null;\\r\\n  /** The one booking link that matches this student\'s plan. */\\r\\n  booking: HomeBookingCta | null;\\r\\n  week: HomeWeekDay[];\\r\\n  weekLabel: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ── small formatters ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\\r\\n\\r\\nconst ONES = [\\r\\n  \\"zero\\", \\"one\\", \\"two\\", \\"three\\", \\"four\\", \\"five\\",\\r\\n  \\"six\\", \\"seven\\", \\"eight\\", \\"nine\\", \\"ten\\", \\"eleven\\", \\"twelve\\",\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Editorial voice: small numbers read as words in prose, digits everywhere else. */\\r\\nexport function spell(n: number): string {\\r\\n  return n >= 0 && n < ONES.length ? ONES[n] : String(n);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function greetingFor(now: Date = new Date()): string {\\r\\n  const hour = now.getHours();\\r\\n  if (hour < 12) return \\"Good morning\\";\\r\\n  if (hour < 18) return \\"Good afternoon\\";\\r\\n  return \\"Good evening\\";\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** \\"MONDAY · AUGUST 3 · 2026\\" — three beats, with the date kept whole. */\\r\\nexport function datelineFor(now: Date = new Date()): string {\\r\\n  const part = (options: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions) =>\\r\\n    new Intl.DateTimeFormat(\\"en-CA\\", options).format(now);\\r\\n\\"use client\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nimport * as React from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { createPortal } from \\"react-dom\\";\\r\\nimport { useNavigate } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  ArrowRight,\\r\\n  CornerDownLeft,\\r\\n  LayoutGrid,\\r\\n  Loader2,\\r\\n  Search,\\r\\n  X,\\r\\n} from \\"lucide-react\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  formatQuestionAddress,\\r\\n  lrHitHref,\\r\\n  rcHitHref,\\r\\n  runFinderSearch,\\r\\n  type FinderHit,\\r\\n  type FinderSnippet,\\r\\n  type QuestionFinderBank,\\r\\n} from \\"./questionFinderSearch\\";\\r\\nimport { useQuestionFinderBanks } from \\"./useQuestionFinderBanks\\";\\r\\nimport \\"./questionFinder.css\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst PER_BANK_LIMIT = 6;\\r\\n\\r\\n/* The bank switch, as a segmented control rather than a hidden filter.\\r\\n   LSAT Lab puts \\"Modern | Legacy\\" here, which splits its bank by PrepTest\\r\\n   numbering. Ours is PT101-159 end to end — every question is modern — so a\\r\\n   Legacy tab would be a control that returns nothing every time it is pressed.\\r\\n   The switch that is real here is the one between the two banks. */\\r\\nconst BANK_TABS: { value: QuestionFinderBank | \\"all\\"; label: string }[] = [\\r\\n  { value: \\"all\\", label: \\"All\\" },\\r\\n  { value: \\"lr\\", label: \\"LR\\" },\\r\\n  { value: \\"rc\\", label: \\"RC\\" },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface QuestionFinderProps {\\r\\n  /** Where a finished one-question attempt returns to. */\\r\\n  returnTo: string;\\r\\n  /** The host\'s surface. The panel follows it so the overlay never flashes a\\r\\n   *  warm-paper card over the near-black Drill landing workspace. */\\r\\n  tone?: \\"light\\" | \\"dark\\";\\r\\n  /** Restricts results to the active side of the LR/RC hub switch. */\\r\\n  bank?: QuestionFinderBank;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The Question finder.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A labelled field that opens a panel: type words to search question text, or\\r\\n * type a question address (PT90 S1 Q12) to go straight there. It can search\\r\\n * both banks or follow the Drill hub\'s active bank. LR and RC question hits\\r\\n * both open the exact question; RC passage matches open the matching passage.\\r\\n * See `docs/adr/0008-question-finder-lands-differently-by-bank.md`.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * It never builds a set. That is the Drill console\'s job, and the last row of\\r\\n * the panel is the door to it.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function QuestionFinder({ returnTo, tone = \\"light\\", bank }: QuestionFinderProps) {\\r\\n  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /* Cmd/Ctrl+K is an accelerator on top of a visible, labelled control — never\\r\\n     the only way in. Next: [Assignment name], due Friday.\', action: \'See assigned work\' }\\r\\n    ];\\r\\n    var dot = function (c) { return \'width: 8px; height: 8px; border-radius: 50%; margin-top: 6px; flex-shrink: 0; background: \' + c + \';\'; };\\r\\n    var inbox = [\\r\\n      { title: \'New assignment\', sub: \'[Assignment name] · due Friday\', time: \'9:40\', dot: dot(\'#ff8906\') },\\r\\n      { title: \'Section graded\', sub: \'Read, Recall, Colour · report card ready\', time: \'Tue\', dot: dot(\'#ff8906\') },\\r\\n      { title: \'Session tomorrow\', sub: \'Thursday 7:15 p.m. · reminder\', time: \'Tue\', dot: dot(\'#d6d1cb\') },\\r\\n      { title: \'Recap posted\', sub: \'Notes from Monday’s session\', time: \'Mon\', dot: dot(\'#d6d1cb\') },\\r\\n      { title: \'Booking moved\', sub: \'Now Thursday 7:15 p.m.\', time: \'Sun\', dot: dot(\'#d6d1cb\') }\\r\\n    ];\\r\\n    var days = [[\'WED\', 23, \'Open\', \'today\'], [\'THU\', 24, \'Open\', \'\'], [\'FRI\', 25, \'Open\', \'\'], [\'SAT\', 26, \'Rest\', \'rest\'], [\'SUN\', 27, \'Rest\', \'rest\'], [\'MON\', 28, \'Open\', \'\'], [\'TUE\', 29, \'Open\', \'\']];\\r\\n    var week = days.map(function (d) {\\r\\n      var today = d[3] === \'today\', rest = d[3] === \'rest\';\\r\\n      return {\\r\\n        abbr: d[0], num: d[1], label: d[2],\\r\\n        card: \'display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; padding: 14px; border-radius: 14px; \' + (today ? \'background: #44403d;\' : rest ? \'background: rgba(255,255,255,0.45); box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(68,64,61,0.05);\' : \'background: #fff; box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(68,64,61,0.08);\'),\\r\\n        abbrStyle: \\"font-family: \'JetBrains Mono\', monospace; font-size: 10px; letter-spacing: 0.14em; color: \\" + (today ? \'#d6d1cb\' : rest ? \'#d6d1cb\' : \'#a7a29f\') + \';\',\\r\\n        numStyle: \'font-family: Fraunces, serif; font-size: 22px; color: \' + (today ? \'#fff\' : rest ? \'#d6d1cb\' : \'#332f2c\') + \';\',\\r\\n        labelStyle: \'font-size: 13px; color: \' + (today ? \'#fff\' : rest ? \'#d6d1cb\' : \'#8a857f\') + \';\'\\r\\n      };\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    return { minor: minor, inbox: inbox, week: week };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n</script>\\r\\n</body>\\r\\n</html>\\r\\n/* ── Classroom Home ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n   The student dashboard. Hierarchy is the point — one primary\\r\\n   action, then the work, then the coach and the next session, then the week.\\r\\n\\r\\n   The Classroom\'s warm palette is a deliberate, scoped exception to the app\'s\\r\\n   monochrome editorial system. Behind everything, inert to the pointer. */\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-glow {\\r\\n  border-radius: 50%;\\r\\n  filter: blur(24px);\\r\\n  pointer-events: none;\\r\\n  position: absolute;\\r\\n  z-index: 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-glow-a {\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeDrift 16s var(--home-ease) infinite;\\r\\n  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(255, 137, 6, 0.1), transparent 68%);\\r\\n  height: 420px;\\r\\n  right: -140px;\\r\\n  top: 40px;\\r\\n  width: 620px;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-glow-b {\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeDrift 22s var(--home-ease) 3s infinite reverse;\\r\\n  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(255, 137, 6, 0.055), transparent 68%);\\r\\n  bottom: 40px;\\r\\n  filter: blur(30px);\\r\\n  height: 380px;\\r\\n  right: -80px;\\r\\n  width: 520px;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home > *:not(.classroom-home-glow) {\\r\\n  position: relative;\\r\\n  z-index: 1;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ── masthead ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ── finder bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n   A rule of its own above the greeting, the way LSAT Lab hangs Find A Question\\r\\n   over its dashboard. The trigger brings its own pill shape; this band only\\r\\n   places it and draws the line under it. */\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-finderbar {\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeRise 560ms var(--home-ease) both;\\r\\n  display: flex;\\r\\n  margin-bottom: 26px;\\r\\n  padding-bottom: 18px;\\r\\n  position: relative;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-finderbar::after {\\r\\n  background: rgba(68, 64, 61, 0.08);\\r\\n  bottom: 0;\\r\\n  content: \\"\\";\\r\\n  height: 1px;\\r\\n  left: 0;\\r\\n  position: absolute;\\r\\n  right: 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-finderbar .qf-trigger {\\r\\n  min-width: 264px;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* The trigger drops its label on a narrow screen wherever it shares a row.\\r\\n   Here it does not share one — the band is its own — and an unlabelled circle\\r\\n   alone above the greeting reads as decoration rather than the way in. So the\\r\\n   words stay and the pill takes the width instead. */\\r\\n@media (max-width: 720px) {\\r\\n  .classroom-root .classroom-home-finderbar .qf-trigger {\\r\\n    min-width: 0;\\r\\n    padding: 10px 12px 10px 14px;\\r\\n    width: 100%;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  .classroom-root .classroom-home-finderbar .qf-trigger-label {\\r\\n    display: block;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-masthead {\\r\\n  align-items: flex-end;\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeRise 560ms var(--home-ease) 40ms both;\\r\\n  display: flex;\\r\\n  gap: 40px;\\r\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-masthead-text {\\r\\n  display: flex;\\r\\n  flex-direction: column;\\r\\n  gap: 10px;\\r\\n  min-width: 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-eyebrow {\\r\\n  align-items: center;\\r\\n  color: var(--home-muted);\\r\\n  display: flex;\\r\\n  font-family: var(--home-mono);\\r\\n  font-size: 10.5px;\\r\\n  font-weight: 600;\\r\\n  gap: 10px;\\r\\n  letter-spacing: 0.16em;\\r\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-eyebrow-dot {\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeUnread 2.8s var(--home-ease) 1s infinite;\\r\\n  background: var(--home-accent);\\r\\n  border-radius: 50%;\\r\\n  flex: none;\\r\\n  height: 5px;\\r\\n  width: 5px;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-title {\\r\\n  color: var(--home-ink);\\r\\n  font-family: var(--home-display);\\r\\n  font-size: 42px;\\r\\n  font-weight: 600;\\r\\n  letter-spacing: -0.02em;\\r\\n  line-height: 1.05;\\r\\n  margin: 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-subtitle {\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeRise 560ms var(--home-ease) 140ms both;\\r\\n  color: var(--home-body);\\r\\n  font-family: var(--home-display);\\r\\n  font-size: 16px;\\r\\n  font-style: italic;\\r\\n  line-height: 1.55;\\r\\n  margin: 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* The masthead\'s utility cluster: status only, now that the address controls\\r\\n   have their own band below. A row of its own, so what sits here cannot change\\r\\n   where the pill sits relative to the greeting. */\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-masthead-tools {\\r\\n  align-items: center;\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeRise 560ms var(--home-ease) 100ms both;\\r\\n  display: flex;\\r\\n  flex: none;\\r\\n  gap: 12px;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-duepill {\\r\\n  align-items: center;\\r\\n  background: var(--home-white);\\r\\n  border-radius: 999px;\\r\\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--home-hair-08), 0 6px 16px -12px rgba(68, 64, 61, 0.4);\\r\\n  color: var(--home-body);\\r\\n  display: flex;\\r\\n  flex: none;\\r\\n  font-family: var(--home-mono);\\r\\n  font-size: 10px;\\r\\n  font-weight: 600;\\r\\n  gap: 9px;\\r\\n  letter-spacing: 0.14em;\\r\\n  padding: 9px 14px;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-duepill i {\\r\\n  animation: classroomHomeUnread 2.8s var(--home-ease) 1s infinite;\\r\\n  background: var(--home-accent);\\r\\n  border-radius: 50%;\\r\\n  height: 6px;\\r\\n  width: 6px;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ── hero ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\\r\\n\\r\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-hero {\\r\\n---\\r\\nname: browser-harness\\r\\ndescription: \\"Always use browser-harness for any web interaction: automation, scraping, testing, or site/app work.\\"\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# browser-harness\\r\\n\\r\\nDirect browser control via CDP. For task-specific edits, use `agent-workspace/agent_helpers.py`. For setup, install, or connection problems, read https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/install.md.\\r\\n\\r\\nDomain skills are off by default. Set `BH_DOMAIN_SKILLS=1` to enable them; see the bottom section.\\r\\n\\r\\n**If `BH_DOMAIN_SKILLS=1` and the task is site-specific, read every file in the matching `$BH_AGENT_WORKSPACE/domain-skills/<site>/` directory before inventing an approach.**\\r\\n\\r\\n## Usage\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nbrowser-harness <<\'PY\'\\r\\nprint(page_info())\\r\\nPY\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n- Invoke as `browser-harness`. Use heredocs for multi-line commands.\\r\\n- Helpers are pre-imported. `run.py` calls `ensure_daemon()` before `exec`.\\r\\n- First navigation is `new_tab(url)`, not `goto_url(url)`.\\r\\n- The normal local flow attaches to the running Chrome/Chromium CDP endpoint. No browser ids or local profile selection.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Local Chrome\\r\\n\\r\\nIf the daemon cannot connect, run diagnostics:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nbrowser-harness --doctor\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nIf Chrome remote debugging is not enabled, the harness opens:\\r\\n\\r\\n```text\\r\\nchrome://inspect/#remote-debugging\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk the user to tick \\"Allow remote debugging for this browser instance\\" and click Allow if Chrome shows a permission popup. Then retry the same `browser-harness` command.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Remote Browsers\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Browser Use cloud for headless servers, parallel sub-agents, or isolated work. Authenticate once:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nbrowser-harness auth login\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nOr import a key safely:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nbrowser-harness auth login --api-key-stdin\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nPick a short made-up name; `r7k2` below is just a placeholder:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nbrowser-harness <<\'PY\'\\r\\nstart_remote_daemon(\\"r7k2\\")\\r\\nPY\\r\\n\\r\\nBU_NAME=r7k2 browser-harness <<\'PY\'\\r\\nnew_tab(\\"https://example.com\\")\\r\\nprint(page_info())\\r\\nPY\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nWhen the task is done and a cloud browser is still running, ask directly: \\"Should I close this browser now?\\" If yes, run `stop_remote_daemon(name)`. Remote daemons bill until they stop or time out.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not start a remote daemon and then keep using the default daemon. Use the same name for `BU_NAME`.\\r\\n\\r\\nCloud profile cookie sync reference: https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/interaction-skills/profile-sync.md.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Page Workflow\\r\\n\\r\\n- Screenshots first: use `capture_screenshot()` to understand visible state.\\r\\n- Clicking: screenshot -> read pixel -> `click_at_xy(x, y)` -> screenshot again.\\r\\n- After navigation, call `wait_for_load()`.\\r\\n- If the current tab is stale or internal, call `ensure_real_tab()`.\\r\\n- Use `js(...)` for DOM inspection or extraction when coordinates are the wrong tool.\\r\\n- Login walls: stop and ask. Exception: use available SSO automatically when Chrome is already signed in; still stop for passwords, MFA, consent, or ambiguous account choice.\\r\\n- Raw CDP is available with `cdp(\\"Domain.method\\", ...)`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Interaction Skills\\r\\n\\r\\nIf you get stuck on a browser mechanic, check https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/tree/main/interaction-skills.\\r\\n\\r\\n- connection.md\\r\\n- cookies.md\\r\\n- cross-origin-iframes.md\\r\\n- dialogs.md\\r\\n- downloads.md\\r\\n- drag-and-drop.md\\r\\n- dropdowns.md\\r\\n- iframes.md\\r\\n- network-requests.md\\r\\n- print-as-pdf.md\\r\\n- profile-sync.md\\r\\n- screenshots.md\\r\\n- scrolling.md\\r\\n- shadow-dom.md\\r\\n- tabs.md\\r\\n- uploads.md\\r\\n- viewport.md\\r\\n\\r\\n## Design Constraints\\r\\n\\r\\n- Coordinate clicks default. CDP mouse events pass through iframes/shadow/cross-origin at the compositor level.\\r\\n- Keep the connection model simple: use the default daemon, `BU_NAME`, `BU_CDP_URL`, `BU_CDP_WS`, or `start_remote_daemon(...)`.\\r\\n- Core helpers stay short. Put task-specific helper additions in `$BH_AGENT_WORKSPACE/agent_helpers.py`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Gotchas\\r\\n\\r\\n- `chrome://inspect/#remote-debugging` must be enabled for local Chrome control.\\r\\n- Chrome may show an \\"Allow remote debugging?\\" popup; wait for the user to click Allow.\\r\\n- Omnibox popups are not real work tabs.\\r\\n- CDP target order is not Chrome\'s visible tab-strip order.\\r\\n- `BU_CDP_URL` is an HTTP DevTools endpoint; the daemon resolves it to WebSocket.\\r\\n- Ask before leaving cloud browsers running; stop them with `stop_remote_daemon(name)` or `PATCH /browsers/{id} {\\"action\\":\\"stop\\"}`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Domain Skills\\r\\n\\r\\nOnly applies when `BH_DOMAIN_SKILLS=1`. Otherwise ignore domain skills.\\r\\n\\r\\nWhen enabled, search `$BH_AGENT_WORKSPACE/domain-skills/<host>/` before inventing an approach. `goto_url(...)` returns up to 10 skill filenames for the navigated host.\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Home already filters out coach assignments and carries the data needed for Phase 1. I’ll preserve those filters, the briefing flow, and message dismissal, and add a way for the timed-section card to open the single existing Question finder. I’m using the browser-harness skill for the visual checks.

She has already done the work; what this offers is the\\r\\n        // part she asked for by name.\\r\\n        actionLabel: \\"See what you missed\\",\\r\\n        emphasis: false,\\r\\n        isNew: true,\\r\\n        dueToday: false,\\r\\n        overdue: false,\\r\\n        // Nothing for her to tick off. [])\\r\\n      .filter(\\r\\n\\r\\n        (item: ClassroomItem) =>\\r\\n          NOTE_TYPES.has(item.type) &&\\r\\n          !isDayZeroWelcome(item) &&\\r\\n          item.status !== \\"archived\\" &&\\r\\n          item.status !== \\"draft\\" &&\\r\\n          new Date(item.created_at).getTime() >= cutoff,\\r\\n      )\\r\\n      .sort((a, b) => {\\r\\n        const unread = Number(isNoteUnread(b)) - Number(isNoteUnread(a));\\r\\n        if (unread !== 0) return unread;\\r\\n        return new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime();\\r\\n      })\\r\\n      .slice(0, 3)\\r\\n      .map((item) => ({\\r\\n        key: `item:${item.id}`,\\r\\n        title: item.title,\\r\\n        kind: \\"recap\\" as HomeKind,\\r\\n        tone: KIND_TONE.recap,\\r\\n        meta: [\\r\\n          KIND_LABEL.recap,\\r\\n          `posted ${new Intl.DateTimeFormat(\\"en-CA\\", {\\r\\n            day: \\"numeric\\",\\r\\n            month: \\"short\\",\\r\\n          }).format(new Date(item.created_at))}`,\\r\\n        ].join(\\" · \\"),\\r\\n        route: `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`,\\r\\n        actionLabel: \\"Read\\",\\r\\n        emphasis: false,\\r\\n        isNew: isNoteUnread(item),\\r\\n        dueToday: false,\\r\\n        overdue: false,\\r\\n      }));\\r\\n  }, [itemsQuery.data, permissions.is_admin]);\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Overdue first, then due today, then the rest newest-first, then reading\\r\\n   * matter. The order is the page\'s argument: the top row is what to do next.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const rows = React.useMemo(() => {\\r\\n    const ranked = [...workRows].sort((a, b) => {\\r\\n      const rank = (row: HomeRow) => (row.overdue ? 0 : row.dueToday ? 1 : 2);\\r\\n      const delta = rank(a) - rank(b);\\r\\n      if (delta !== 0) return delta;\\r\\n      return a.isNew === b.isNew ? 0 : a.isNew ? -1 : 1;\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    // Unread reading matter goes above ordinary work but below anything\\r\\n    // overdue or due today. Appending it after every assignment looked right\\r\\n    // and was not: Home shows four rows, so a student with four open\\r\\n    // assignments never saw a new note at all, which is the case this whole\\r\\n    // feature exists for.\\r\\n    const urgent = ranked.filter((row) => row.overdue || row.dueToday);\\r\\n    const rest = ranked.filter((row) => !(row.overdue || row.dueToday));\\r\\n    const unreadReading = readingRows.filter((row) => row.isNew);\\r\\n    const readReading = readingRows.filter((row) => !row.isNew);\\r\\n    /*\\r\\n     * A ready Report Card sits above ordinary work and below anything overdue.\\r\\n     * Only four rows are drawn, so anything appended to the end is invisible to\\r\\n     * a student with a full week, and this is news with a short life: it is\\r\\n     * worth reading tonight and stale by the weekend.\\r\\n     */\\r\\n    const all = [...urgent, ...reportRows, ...unreadReading, ...rest, ...readReading];\\r\\n    if (all.length) all[0] = { ...all[0], emphasis: true };\\r\\n    return all;\\r\\n  }, [readingRows, reportRows, workRows]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // The hero takes the top row. The list below repeats it deliberately: the\\r\\n  // hero is the argument for doing it now, the list is the inventory, and a\\r\\n  // student scanning only the list should still find the thing that is due.\\r\\n  /*\\r\\n   * Never a Report Card. The hero argues for doing something next and takes its\\r\\n   * button label from the kind of work it is, so a finished run would be\\r\\n   * offered back to her as \\"Start\\". It belongs in the inventory below.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const heroRow = rows.find((row) => !row.key.startsWith(\\"report:\\")) ?? null;\\r\\n  const listRows = rows.slice(0, 4);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const heroActivity = React.useMemo(() => {\\r\\n              ) : null}\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n            <span className=\\"classroom-home-session-actions\\">\\r\\n              {/* No Reschedule pill: neither student-booking nor public-booking\\r\\n                  exposes a reschedule action. The booking pill itself follows\\r\\n                  the student\'s plan — credits go to the in-app picker, a\\r\\n                  pay-per-session student gets their own link. */}\\r\\n              <Pill\\r\\n                emphasis\\r\\n                onClick={() => {\\r\\n                  const target = model.booking?.href ?? \\"/booking\\";\\r\\n                  if (model.booking?.external) window.open(target, \\"_blank\\", \\"noopener,noreferrer\\");\\r\\n                  else handleOpen(target);\\r\\n                }}\\r\\n              >\\r\\n                {model.booking?.label ?? (model.session ? \\"Book another\\" : \\"Book one\\")}\\r\\n              </Pill>\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n          </section>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* ── this week ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\\r\\n      <section className=\\"classroom-home-week\\" aria-label=\\"Next seven days\\">\\r\\n        <header className=\\"classroom-home-rule\\">\\r\\n          <span className=\\"classroom-home-card-label\\">NEXT SEVEN DAYS</span>\\r\\n          <i className=\\"classroom-home-rule-draw\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n          <span className=\\"classroom-home-week-range\\">{model.weekLabel}</span>\\r\\n        </header>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"classroom-home-days\\">\\r\\n          {model.week.map((day) => {\\r\\n            const tone = day.isToday\\r\\n              ? \\" classroom-home-day-today\\"\\r\\n              : day.label\\r\\n                ? \\" classroom-home-day-item\\"\\r\\n                : day.isWeekend\\r\\n                  ? \\" classroom-home-day-rest\\"\\r\\n                  : \\" classroom-home-day-open\\";\\r\\n            const body = (\\r\\n              <>\\r\\n                <span className=\\"classroom-home-day-head\\">\\r\\n                  <span className=\\"classroom-home-day-abbr\\">{day.abbr}</span>\\r\\n                  <span className=\\"classroom-home-day-num\\">{day.dayNum}</span>\\r\\n                </span>\\r\\n                <span className=\\"classroom-home-day-label\\">\\r\\n                  {day.label ? (\\r\\n                    <>\\r\\n                      <i aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n                      {day.label}\\r\\n                    </>\\r\\n                  ) : day.isWeekend ? (\\r\\n                    \\"Rest\\"\\r\\n                  ) : (\\r\\n                    \\"Open\\"\\r\\n                  )}\\r\\n                </span>\\r\\n              </>\\r\\n            );\\r\\n\\r\\n            // The band still reads without schedule access; it just stops being\\r\\n            // a control, rather than becoming a button onto a locked route.\\r\\n            return model.access.schedule ? (\\r\\n              <button\\r\\n                key={day.iso}\\r\\n                type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                className={`classroom-home-day${tone}`}\\r\\n                onClick={() => handleOpen(\\"/schedule\\")}\\r\\n              >\\r\\n                {body}\\r\\n              </button>\\r\\n            ) : (\\r\\n              <div key={day.iso} className={`classroom-home-day${tone}`}>\\r\\n                {body}\\r\\n              </div>\\r\\n            );\\r\\n          })}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </section>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default ClassroomHomeView;\\r\\nimport * as React from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { describe, expect, it, vi } from \\"vitest\\";\\r\\nimport { fireEvent, render as rtlRender, screen, waitFor } from \\"@testing-library/react\\";\\r\\nimport { MemoryRouter } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\r\\nimport { ClassroomHomeView } from \\"./ClassroomHomeView\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  buildWeek,\\r\\n  datelineFor,\\r\\n  dueLabel,\\r\\n  stripKind,\\r\\n  subtitleFor,\\r\\n  weekDates,\\r\\n  type ClassroomHomeModel,\\r\\n} from \\"./homeModel\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/* Home never mounts outside a router, and since the address row navigates on\\r\\n   pick it now says so: `useNavigate` throws without one. Drill whatever you feel like.\\",\\r\\n  dueTodayCount: 0,\\r\\n  hero: null,\\r\\n  rows: [],\\r\\n  newCount: 0,\\r\\n  message: null,\\r\\n  messages: [],\\r\\n  unreadMessages: 0,\\r\\n  session: null,\\r\\n  booking: { label: \\"Book one\\", href: \\"/booking\\", external: false, detail: null },\\r\\n  week: buildWeek(weekDates(new Date(2026, 7, 3)), [], new Date(2026, 7, 3)),\\r\\n  weekLabel: \\"August 3 to 9\\",\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nconst FULL: ClassroomHomeModel = {\\r\\n  ...EMPTY,\\r\\n  subtitle: \\"One assignment is due today.\\",\\r\\n  dueTodayCount: 1,\\r\\n  hero: argument.\\",\\r\\n    primaryLabel: \\"Start the activity\\",\\r\\n    progress: { completed: 1, total: 12, unit: \\"prompts\\", estimateMinutes: null },\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  rows: [\\r\\n    {\\r\\n meet.\\",\\r\\n    unread: true,\\r\\n    route: \\"/inbox\\",\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  messages: [\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      id: \\"m\\",\\r\\n      from: \\"Joshua\\",\\r\\n      initial: \\"J\\",\\r\\n      timeLabel: \\"7:52 a.m.\\",\\r\\n      body: \\"Do the twelve before we meet.\\",\\r\\n      unread: true,\\r\\n      route: \\"/inbox\\",\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  ],\\r\\n  unreadMessages: 1,\\r\\n
