 },\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\ndescribe(\\"ClassroomHomeView\\", () => {\\r\\n  describe(\\"a timed section\\", () => {\\r\\n    const BRIEFING = {\\r\\n      minutes: 53,\\r\\n      sectionName: \\"Logical Reasoning\\",\\r\\n      href: \\"/drill?mode=full-section&pt=155&section=1&minutes=53&sealed=1\\",\\r\\n      sealed: true,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    const sectionRow = {\\r\\n      ...FULL.rows[0],\\r\\n      key: \\"sec\\",\\r\\n      title: \\"Logical Reasoning · Timed Section\\",\\r\\n      kind: \\"homework\\" as const,\\r\\n      tone: \\"drill\\" as const,\\r\\n      route: \\"/classroom/item/abc\\",\\r\\n      briefing: BRIEFING,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    const model = {\\r\\n      ...FULL,\\r\\n      rows: [sectionRow],\\r\\n      hero: { ...FULL.hero!, primaryLabel: \\"Open the set\\", row: sectionRow },\\r\\n    };\\r\\n\\r\\n    it(\\"raises the instructions over Home instead of navigating away\\", () => {\\r\\n      const onOpen = vi.fn();\\r\\n      render(\\r\\n        <ClassroomHomeView model={model} loading={false} onOpen={onOpen} onSeeAllWork={vi.fn()} />,\\r\\n      );\\r\\n\\r\\n      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole(\\"button\\", { name: \\"Open the set\\" }));\\r\\n\\r\\n      expect(onOpen).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\\r\\n      expect(screen.getByRole(\\"dialog\\", { name: \\"Logical Reasoning\\" })).toBeTruthy();\\r\\n      expect(screen.getByText(/53 minutes, one sitting/)).toBeTruthy();\\r\\n    });\\r\\n\\r\\n   */\\r\\n> export interface DrillLaunchOptions {\\r\\n    /** Where the runner\'s exit sends the student. Must be an app-relative path. */\\r\\n    returnTo?: string;\\r\\n    /** Heading shown while the drill runs. */\\r\\n    /**\\r\\n>    * Presentation only: render the adaptive Smart-Drill chrome even for a fixed\\r\\n     * queue, so a hand-picked question looks identical to an Auto run.\\r\\n     */\\r\\n    present?: \'smart\';\\r\\n   */\\r\\n> export interface PtLegRef {\\r\\n    runId: string;\\r\\n    legIndex: number;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n   */\\r\\n> export interface RunCarry {\\r\\n    mode: DrillMode;\\r\\n    sealed: boolean;\\r\\n    isGuidedDrill: boolean;\\r\\n  \\r\\n> export type DrillLaunchBody =\\r\\n    /** The app picks. One continuous adaptive run per student. */\\r\\n>   | { kind: \'smart-drill\' }\\r\\n    /** A real PT section, optionally timed and optionally sealed. */\\r\\n    | {\\r\\n        kind: \'section\';\\r\\n  \\r\\n> export type DrillLaunch = DrillLaunchBody & { options?: DrillLaunchOptions };\\r\\n  \\r\\n  /** The kinds whose question list lives in a row we have to read before starting. */\\r\\n  const KINDS_NEEDING_RESOLUTION = new Set([\\r\\n  \\r\\n> export function launchNeedsResolution(launch: DrillLaunch): boolean {\\r\\n    return KINDS_NEEDING_RESOLUTION.has(launch.kind);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  \\r\\n  /** Thrown when a link asks for something that kind of drill cannot have. */\\r\\n> export class DrillLaunchError extends Error {\\r\\n    constructor(message: string) {\\r\\n      super(message);\\r\\n      this.name = \'DrillLaunchError\';\\r\\n   */\\r\\n> export function launchDrill(launch: DrillLaunch): string {\\r\\n    const p = new URLSearchParams();\\r\\n    p.set(\'drill\', launch.kind);\\r\\n  \\r\\n    switch (launch.kind) {\\r\\n>     case \'smart-drill\':\\r\\n        break;\\r\\n      case \'section\':\\r\\n        p.set(\'pt\', String(launch.pt));\\r\\n    switch (kind) {\\r\\n>     case \'smart-drill\':\\r\\n        return { kind, options };\\r\\n  \\r\\n      case \'section\': {\\r\\n   */\\r\\n> export function parseDrillLaunch(searchParams: URLSearchParams): DrillLaunch | null {\\r\\n    if (searchParams.get(\'drill\')) return parseCanonical(searchParams);\\r\\n    return parseLegacy(searchParams);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n   */\\r\\n> export interface DrillHandoverState {\\r\\n    mode?: DrillMode;\\r\\n    config?: FullSectionConfig | TypeDrillConfig;\\r\\n    practicingLabel?: string;\\r\\n   */\\r\\n> export function launchFromRouteState(\\r\\n    state: DrillHandoverState | null | undefined,\\r\\n    ptLeg?: PtLegRef,\\r\\n  ): DrillLaunch | null {\\r\\n    if (state.mode === \'adaptive\') {\\r\\n>     return { kind: \'smart-drill\', options: withOptions };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  \\r\\n    if (state.mode === \'full-section\') {\\r\\n      case \\"messages\\":\\n unlocked && unreadMessages > 0 ? (\\n                        <span className=\\"sr-only\\"> unread messages</span>\\n  const messagesStage =\\n    location.pathname === \\"/inbox\\" || location.pathname.startsWith(\\"/inbox/\\");\\n    drillHub || messagesStage || workspaceStage ? null : pathSection ?? selectedSection;\\n      : messagesStage\\n        : messagesStage\\n          ? \\"classroom-root classroom-root-messages\\"\\n        ) : messagesStage ? (\\n          <main className=\\"classroom-messages-stage\\">\\n---\\r\\ntitle: Teaching Voice\\r\\ntype: concept\\r\\ntags: [lsat-u, voice, pedagogy, content]\\r\\ncreated: 2026-06-10\\r\\nupdated: 2026-07-18\\r\\nstatus: active\\r\\nsubject: Teaching Voice\\r\\naliases: []\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Teaching Voice\\r\\n\\r\\nHow LSAT U sounds, across every surface. This is the canonical voice doc: route all student-facing and brand copy through it. Built from a grill with Joshua (2026-06-13), then tested and enriched against the full corpus of his real correspondence (944 messages he authored, mid-2025 to mid-2026, from the Gmail takeout; 2026-07-18). Claims below marked with a quote are evidenced by that corpus.\\r\\n\\r\\nIt replaces the old two-register / 15-named-tools \\"preserve verbatim\\" profile (deleted 2026-06-10) and folds in the brand/marketing register the earlier draft was missing.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Core voice (every register)\\r\\n\\r\\nThe constant under all three registers below. This is Joshua on the page.\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Quiet authority.** State the value and let it land. Never oversell, never beg, never perform enthusiasm.\\r\\n- **Specific, never generic.** Real scores, the actual skill, the student\'s own history. Praise is a specific observation, not \\"great job.\\"\\r\\n- **Honest about the work, gentle about the person.** Be blunt that the LSAT is hard (\\"expect this to be exhausting and very challenging\\"). Never be blunt about a student\'s shortcomings. Honesty points at the material; warmth points at the person. When someone slips, nudge gently, never accuse.\\r\\n- **Light, grounded metaphor, understated in email.** In his real correspondence the recurring image is quiet: \\"partner with you on your LSAT journey,\\" \\"getting set up,\\" \\"foundational.\\" The vivid images (runway, climb, march toward November, an easy door to walk through) belong to authored teaching and brand copy, not to email. Keep metaphor light and grounded; never float off into abstraction.\\r\\n- **Balanced rhythm.** Short declaratives plus one slightly longer warm beat. Air between ideas. Each idea gets its own line. Real messages are short and single-purpose (median ~280 characters).\\r\\n- **Plain professional diction.** No corporate jargon, no startup hype (\\"unlock,\\" \\"crush,\\" \\"level up,\\" \\"game-changer\\" — \\"crush\\" appears once in 944 messages). Contractions are the norm, not the exception. Exclamation points are genuine, not performed: a warm message often carries one, occasionally more, especially in openers (\\"Sorry for such a long delay!\\"). He is not exclamation-averse.\\r\\n- **Forward-looking.** End on the next step or what\'s ahead.\\r\\n- **No em dashes, ever.** Use periods, colons before lists, the middot (·) as a separator, and numbered steps for procedures.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Openings, closings, and signature phrases\\r\\n\\r\\nThe mechanical fingerprint of his email, from the real corpus. Match these when drafting as Joshua.\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Greeting.** \\"Hi [Name],\\" is the default (used ~519x). \\"Hey [Name],\\" for established/returning students (~229x, warmer). \\"Hello [Name],\\" (~105x) and \\"Dear [Name],\\" (~18x, formal) for first contact or a more formal note.\\r\\n- **Sign-off.** \\"Best,\\" and \\"Best regards,\\" are the workhorses (~360x / ~237x). \\"Thanks,\\" when it\'s a favor or logistics (~146x). \\"Warmly,\\" / \\"Warm regards,\\" (~54x) for a genuinely warm note. \\"Talk soon,\\" (~9x) for someone he\'ll see again soon.\\r\\n- **Signature.** Almost always signed **\\"Joshua\\"** (844 of 944 messages). The name is near-universal; include it.\\r\\n- **Signature phrases (his verbal fingerprint, use naturally, don\'t overuse):** \\"let me know\\" (by far the most common, ~390x), \\"looking forward,\\" \\"I hope\\" / \\"hope all is well\\" / \\"hope you,\\" \\"no worries at all,\\" \\"happy to\\" / \\"more than happy to,\\" \\"just wanted to make sure,\\" \\"make sure,\\" \\"no rush,\\" \\"of course.\\"\\r\\n- **Punctuation he actually uses:** semicolons (correctly, ~41x); ellipses essentially never. (For em-dashes, the hard rule in Core voice governs — never.)\\r\\n\\r\\n## The apology / accountability move\\r\\n\\r\\nOne of his most frequent and characteristic behaviors, and worth naming on its own. When he is late to reply, or missed or mishandled something, he opens by owning it plainly, often with a brief honest reason, then pivots straight to the fix. No over-apologizing, no excuses dressed up.\\r\\n\\r\\n> I\'m so sorry. I thought I had let you know ... I clearly didn\'t communicate that as well as I should have.\\r\\n\\r\\n> I\'ve been away from work for personal reasons, but I\'m back now and getting caught up.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe pattern: own it in the first line, give the real reason in one clause, move to what happens next. Warm, candid, brief.\\r\\n\\r\\n## When something on his side broke (approved shape, 2026-09-11)\\r\\n\\r\\nFrom the Sandra booking-error reply, edited by Joshua line by line over three drafts and then sent as-is. Use this shape whenever a student hit a bug or a mistake that was his. The sent text is the reference:\\r\\n\\r\\n> Hi Sandra,\\r\\n>\\r\\n> I\'m sorry about this. The error was on my end, not yours.\\r\\n>\\r\\n> To confirm: none of your bookings went through, so nothing is on the calendar yet. The page was showing a few times that the system then refused, and it looks like you landed on those. That is fixed now.\\r\\n>\\r\\n> Your link is the same one as before:\\r\\n> [link]\\r\\n>\\r\\n> Pick any time it shows and it will book. If it\'s easier, just send me the time you were trying for and I\'ll add it to the schedule myself.\\r\\n>\\r\\n> If you run into any other issues, let me know.\\r\\n>\\r\\n> Once again, my apologies.\\r\\n>\\r\\n> Best,\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"2e7405","wall_time_seconds":2.0124867,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1413,"output":"// Local calendar-date helpers.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Day boundaries for streaks and daily stats must follow the student\'s local\\r\\n// timezone, not UTC. `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` returns the UTC\\r\\n// calendar date, so any evening practice in a US timezone lands under\\r\\n// \\"tomorrow\\" and breaks streak/day-boundary math. Use these instead.\\r\\n\\r\\n// Format a Date as its LOCAL YYYY-MM-DD (en-CA yields ISO-style order).\\r\\nexport function localDayISO(date: Date = new Date()): string {\\r\\n  const y = date.getFullYear();\\r\\n  const m = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, \'0\');\\r\\n  const d = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, \'0\');\\r\\n  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// Parse a YYYY-MM-DD calendar date into that day\'s LOCAL midnight.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// `new Date(\'2026-08-05\')` is parsed as UTC midnight, which in any western\\r\\n// timezone is the evening of the 4th. Scheduling work for \\"the 5th\\" that way\\r\\n// unlocks it a day early. Splitting the parts and using the Date(y, m, d)\\r\\n// constructor keeps the day the day the teacher picked.\\r\\nexport function parseLocalDay(iso: string): Date | null {\\r\\n  const match = /^(\\\\d{4})-(\\\\d{2})-(\\\\d{2})$/.exec(iso.trim());\\r\\n  if (!match) return null;\\r\\n  const [, y, m, d] = match;\\r\\n  const date = new Date(Number(y), Number(m) - 1, Number(d));\\r\\n  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// Local date N days offset from `from`, formatted YYYY-MM-DD. Uses setDate so\\r\\n// it stays correct across DST transitions (unlike subtracting 86_400_000 ms).\\r\\nexport function addLocalDays(days: number, from: Date = new Date()): string {\\r\\n  const d = new Date(from);\\r\\n  d.setDate(d.getDate() + days);\\r\\n  return localDayISO(d);\\r\\n}\\r\\n  loading: boolean;\\r\\n  /** A read failed. Never report \\"nothing assigned\\" on a failed read. */\\r\\n  failed: boolean;\\r\\n  retry: () => void;\\r\\n} {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const { assignments: hw, loading: hwLoading, error: hwError, refresh: hwRefresh } = useStudentAssignments();\\r\\n  const { assignments: ta, loading: taLoading, error: taError, refresh: taRefresh } = useStudentTAAssignments();\\r\\n  const itemsQuery = useClassroomItems();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: guided = [], isLoading: guidedLoading, isError: guidedError, refetch: guidedRefetch } = useQuery<GuidedRow[]>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'todo-feed:guided\', user?.id],\\r\\n    enabled: !!user,\\r\\n    staleTime: 30_000,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => (await listGuidedForStudent(user!.id)) as GuidedRow[],\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: activities = [], isLoading: actLoading, isError: actError, refetch: actRefetch } = useQuery({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'todo-feed:activity\', user?.id],\\r\\n    enabled: !!user,\\r\\n    staleTime: 30_000,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => listActivityForStudent(user!.id),\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const items = itemsQuery.data ?? [];\\r\\n  const rows = useMemo(\\r\\n    () => buildAssignedRows({ hw, ta, guided, activities, items }),\\r\\n    [hw, ta, guided, activities, items],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const retry = useMemo(\\r\\n    () => () => {\\r\\n      void hwRefresh();\\r\\n      void taRefresh();\\r\\n      void guidedRefetch();\\r\\n      void actRefetch();\\r\\n      void itemsQuery.refetch();\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [hwRefresh, taRefresh, guidedRefetch, actRefetch, itemsQuery],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    rows,\\r\\n    loading: hwLoading || taLoading || guidedLoading || actLoading || itemsQuery.isLoading,\\r\\n    failed: !!hwError || !!taError || guidedError || actError || itemsQuery.isError,\\r\\n    retry,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\nrg: src/lib/assigned*: The filename, directory name, or volume label syntax is incorrect. (os error 123)\\nrg: src/hooks/useAssigned*: The filename, directory name, or volume label syntax is incorrect. from \'@/lib/sectionBriefing\';\\r\\nimport { singleSectionOf } from \'@/lib/drillRunSpec\';\\r\\nimport { sectionDirectionsRoute } from \'@/lib/sectionDirections\';\\r\\nimport { listAssignmentsForStudent as listGuidedForStudent, type GuidedDrill, type GuidedDrillAssignment } from \'@/lib/guidedDrills\';\\r\\nimport { listStudentAssignments as listActivityForStudent } from \'@/features/activity-centre/api/activitiesClient\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * A unified read-only \\"assigned\\" row surfaced in the student\'s Today list.\\r\\n * Status is derived from the underlying source row so completing work on the\\r\\n * assignment page reflects here without any extra plumbing.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface AssignedTodoRow {\\r\\n  key: string;\\r\\n  kind: \'homework\' | \'ta\' | \'activity\' | \'guided\' | \'bootcamp\' | \'plan\';\\r\\n  refId: string;\\r\\n  /** The table `refId` points into, so a classroom_items row can be matched to it. */\\r\\n  sourceTable: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  subtitle: string;\\r\\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\';\\r\\n  createdAt: string;\\r\\n  /** Due date, when a classroom_items row carries one. */\\r\\n  dueAt: string | null;\\r\\n  route: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Work that reports its own completion, so the student is never asked to\\r\\n   * assert it. A timed section is the case: sitting it IS finishing it, and a\\r\\n   * \\"Mark done\\" button beside one can be pressed without doing the section at\\r\\n   * all, which is worse than useless on work assigned as a measurement.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  selfCompleting?: boolean;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Present only on a timed section. Home opens these instructions over the\\r\\n   * stage instead of navigating, so declining costs the student nothing.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  briefing?: SectionBriefing;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ntype GuidedRow = GuidedDrillAssignment & { guided_drill: GuidedDrill };\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction normalizeStatus(raw: string | null | undefined): AssignedTodoRow[\'status\'] {\\r\\n  if (raw === \'completed\') return \'completed\';\\r\\n  if (raw === \'assigned\') return \'assigned\';\\r\\n  return \'in_progress\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** classroom_items types that represent work rather than reading material. */\\r\\nconst WORK_ITEM_KIND: Partial<Record<ClassroomItem[\'type\'], AssignedTodoRow[\'kind\']>> = {\\r\\n  drill: \'guided\',\\r\\n  homework: \'homework\',\\r\\n  activity: \'activity\',\\r\\n  bootcamp_module: \'bootcamp\',\\r\\n  weekly_plan_task: \'plan\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * `/guided/:id` reads `returnTo` to decide where finishing the drill lands the\\r\\n * student. Building it into the row would bake in whichever surface happened to\\r\\n * construct the feed, which is how every guided drill ended up returning to\\r\\n * `/schedule` no matter where it was launched from. Consumers call this at\\r\\n * click time with their own location instead.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function routeWithReturnTo(route: string, from: string): string {\\r\\n  if (!route.startsWith(\'/guided/\')) return route;\\r\\n  const [path, query = \'\'] = route.split(\'?\');\\r\\n  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);\\r\\n  params.set(\'returnTo\', from);\\r\\n  return `${path}?${params.toString()}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The single source for \\"what has been assigned to me\\".\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Two independent builders used to answer this question — this hook, and\\r\\n * `buildWorkRows` in classroomData.ts — reading overlapping but different\\r\\n * tables. The result was not merely inconsistent, it was disjoint: Home listed\\r\\n * guided drills and no bootcamp, the Classroom\'s Assigned work panel listed the\\r\\n * bootcamp and no drills. Everything now merges here.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Identity is `sourceTable:refId`. A classroom_items row that points at a\\r\\n * source assignment is the preferred representation of it — the teacher can\\r\\n * retitle it and set a due date — so it overrides, and `resolveItemRoute` sends\\r\\n * it to the same destination the raw row would have used.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface AssignedFeedSources {\\r\\n  hw: ReturnType<typeof useStudentAssignments>[\'assignments\'];\\r\\n  ta: ReturnType<typeof useStudentTAAssignments>[\'assignments\'];\\r\\n  guided: GuidedRow[];\\r\\n  activities: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listActivityForStudent>>;\\r\\n  items: ClassroomItem[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The pure merge behind the hook, exported so the disjoint-feed and\\r\\n * duplicate-assignment rules can be tested without standing up five queries.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function buildAssignedRows({\\r\\n  hw,\\r\\n  ta,\\r\\n  guided,\\r\\n  activities,\\r\\n  items,\\r\\n}: AssignedFeedSources): AssignedTodoRow[] {\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    const byIdentity = new Map<string, AssignedTodoRow>();\\r\\n    const put = (row: AssignedTodoRow) => byIdentity.set(`${row.sourceTable}:${row.refId}`, row);\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const a of hw) {\\r\\n      // A set that is exactly one PrepTest section is sat, not worked through:\\r\\n      // it opens on the section\'s directions page and the clock does the\\r\\n      // rest. Finishing the run completes the row, so it must never carry a\\r\\n      // \\"Mark done\\" the student could press without sitting it.\\r\\n      const section = singleSectionOf(a.question_qids ?? []);\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `hw-${a.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'homework\',\\r\\n        refId: a.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'homework_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: a.set_title || \'Homework\',\\r\\n        subtitle: `${a.question_qids?.length ?? 0} questions${a.score != null ? ` · ${Math.round(a.score)}%` : \'\'}`,\\r\\n        status: normalizeStatus(a.status),\\r\\n        createdAt: a.created_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: section ? sectionDirectionsRoute(a.id) : `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\\r\\n        selfCompleting: !!section,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const a of ta) {\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `ta-${a.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'ta\',\\r\\n        refId: a.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'ta_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: a.title || \'From Joshua\',\\r\\n        subtitle: a.pdf_url ? \'PDF attached\' : \'TA assignment\',\\r\\n        status: a.status === \'viewed\' ? \'in_progress\' : normalizeStatus(a.status),\\r\\n        createdAt: a.assigned_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: `/classroom/ta/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Assigning the same drill twice writes two rows that render identically,\\r\\n    // so they collapse to one. An open assignment always wins over a completed\\r\\n    // one — otherwise finishing the older twin would hide work still owed —\\r\\n    // and the newest wins within that.\\r\\n    const bestByDrill = new Map<string, GuidedRow>();\\r\\n    for (const g of guided) {\\r\\n      const drillId = g.guided_drill_id ?? g.id;\\r\\n      const held = bestByDrill.get(drillId);\\r\\n      if (!held) {\\r\\n        bestByDrill.set(drillId, g);\\r\\n        continue;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      const heldOpen = held.status !== \'completed\';\\r\\n      const thisOpen = g.status !== \'completed\';\\r\\n      if (thisOpen !== heldOpen) {\\r\\n        if (thisOpen) bestByDrill.set(drillId, g);\\r\\n        continue;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (new Date(g.assigned_at).getTime() > new Date(held.assigned_at).getTime()) {\\r\\n        bestByDrill.set(drillId, g);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    for (const g of bestByDrill.values()) {\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `g-${g.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'guided\',\\r\\n        refId: g.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'guided_drill_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: g.guided_drill?.title || \'Guided drill\',\\r\\n        subtitle: `${g.guided_drill?.question_count ?? \'?\'} questions${g.guided_drill?.estimated_minutes ? ` · ${g.guided_drill.estimated_minutes} min` : \'\'}`,\\r\\n        status: g.status === \'completed\' ? \'completed\' : g.status === \'assigned\' ? \'assigned\' : \'in_progress\',\\r\\n        createdAt: g.assigned_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(g.id)}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const a of activities) {\\r\\n      put({\\r\\n        key: `act-${a.id}`,\\r\\n        kind: \'activity\',\\r\\n        refId: a.id,\\r\\n        sourceTable: \'activity_assignments\',\\r\\n        title: a.title || \'Activity\',\\r\\n        subtitle: a.subtitle || \'Activity\',\\r\\n        status: normalizeStatus(a.status),\\r\\n        createdAt: a.created_at,\\r\\n        dueAt: null,\\r\\n        route: `/activities/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const item of items) {\\r\\n      const kind = WORK_ITEM_KIND[item.type];\\r\\n      if (!kind) continue; // notes and recaps are reading material, not work\\r\\n      const route = resolveItemRoute(item);\\r\\n      if (!route) continue;\\r\\n      const sourceTable = item.source_table ?? text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"$env:PYTHONUTF8='1'\n@'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport subprocess,re\np=Path('src/components/classroom/home/home.css'); s=p.read_text().replace('  text-align: left;\\n  font-size: 13px;\\n  transition: transform','  text-align: left;\\n  font-size: 13px;\\n  font-weight: 400;\\n  transition: transform').replace('  font-size: 24px;\\n  line-height: 1.2;','  font-size: 24px;\\n  font-weight: 400;\\n  line-height: 1.2;'); s += '\\n.classroom-root .classroom-home-hero-panel .classroom-home-tick:not(.classroom-home-tick-done) { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, .18); }\\n'; p.write_text(s)\npath='src/components/classroom/home/ClassroomHomeView.tsx'\nold=subprocess.check_output(['git','show','HEAD:'+path]); new=Path(path).read_bytes()\n# Git stores LF; compare the original block with worktree line endings normalized.\nold=old.replace(b'\\r\\n',b'\\n'); new=new.replace(b'\\r\\n',b'\\n')\na=b'      <section className=\"classroom-home-week\"'; end=b'\\n      </section>'\ndef strip(data):\n start=data.index(a); stop=data.index(end,start)+len(end); return data[start:stop]\nassert strip(old)==strip(new)\npath='src/components/classroom/home/home.css'; old=subprocess.check_output(['git','show','HEAD:'+path]).decode(); new=Path(path).read_text()\n# Every existing CSS rule mentioning the week/day selectors must match exactly.\npattern=r'[^{}]*classroom-home-(?:week|day)[^{}]*\\{[^{}]*\\}'\nassert re.findall(pattern,old)==re.findall(pattern,new)\nprint('PASS: Next seven days JSX and every existing week/day CSS rule unchanged (line endings normalized).')\n'@ | python -\ngit diff --check",max_output_tokens:1700}));

Never needs to read anything: writing a\\r\\n * link is always possible from the launch alone, which is what lets `/drill`\\r\\n * canonicalize its own URL on arrival.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Returns the query string without a leading `?`.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function launchDrill(launch: DrillLaunch): string {\\r\\n  const p = new URLSearchParams();\\r\\n  p.set(\'drill\', launch.kind);\\r\\n\\r\\n  switch (launch.kind) {\\r\\n    case \'smart-drill\':\\r\\n      break;\\r\\n    case \'section\':\\r\\n      p.set(\'pt\', String(launch.pt));\\r\\n      p.set(\'section\', String(launch.section));\\r\\n      if (launch.timerMode) p.set(\'timer\', launch.timerMode);\\r\\n      if (launch.minutes) p.set(\'minutes\', String(launch.minutes));\\r\\n      if (launch.sealed) p.set(\'sealed\', \'1\');\\r\\n      if (launch.ptLeg) {\\r\\n        p.set(\'runId\', launch.ptLeg.runId);\\r\\n        p.set(\'leg\', String(launch.ptLeg.legIndex));\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      break;\\r\\n    case \'saved-set\':\\r\\n      p.set(\'set\', launch.setId);\\r\\n      break;\\r\\n    case \'assignment\':\\r\\n      p.set(\'assignment\', launch.assignmentId);\\r\\n      if
